0637 — olympic barbell triceps extension

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 0637 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: source_complete_for_storyboard. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

Holding an Olympic barbell overhead, you bend and straighten your elbows. Isolates the triceps.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Start by standing with your feet shoulder-width apart and holding the barbell with an overhand grip.

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Start by standing with your feet shoulder-width apart and holding the barbell with an overhand grip. 0637.json#/instructions/0
  2. Raise the barbell above your head, fully extending your arms. 0637.json#/instructions/1
  3. Keeping your upper arms close to your head, slowly lower the barbell behind your head by bending your elbows. 0637.json#/instructions/2
  4. Pause for a moment, then extend your arms back to the starting position. 0637.json#/instructions/3
  5. Repeat for the desired number of repetitions. 0637.json#/instructions/4

0638 — one arm chin-up

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 0638 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

An advanced pull-up using only one arm. Builds big lat and biceps strength and takes lots of practice.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Stand facing a pull-up bar with your feet shoulder-width apart.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 0638.json#/instructions/0

Arms Hands

Let the upper arms rest near the torso, elbows soft and hands relaxed; allow small balance movements that do not add a new exercise action.

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 0638.json#/instructions/0

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Stand facing a pull-up bar with your feet shoulder-width apart. 0638.json#/instructions/0
  2. Reach up and grab the bar with an underhand grip, with one hand gripping the bar and the other hand holding your wrist for support. 0638.json#/instructions/1
  3. Hang from the bar with your arm fully extended, keeping your body straight and your core engaged. 0638.json#/instructions/2
  4. Pull yourself up towards the bar by bending your elbow and squeezing your back muscles. 0638.json#/instructions/3
  5. Continue pulling until your chin is above the bar, then slowly lower yourself back down to the starting position. 0638.json#/instructions/4
  6. Repeat for the desired number of repetitions, then switch arms and repeat. 0638.json#/instructions/5

0639 — one arm dip

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 0639 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: source_complete_for_storyboard. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

A dip using one arm on a bench or chair behind you. Works the triceps and shoulders on one side at a time.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Stand facing away from a bench or chair, with your feet shoulder-width apart. Place one hand on the bench or chair behind you, fingers pointing towards your body.

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Stand facing away from a bench or chair, with your feet shoulder-width apart. 0639.json#/instructions/0
  2. Place one hand on the bench or chair behind you, fingers pointing towards your body. 0639.json#/instructions/1
  3. Extend your legs out in front of you, keeping your heels on the ground. 0639.json#/instructions/2
  4. Bend your elbows and lower your body towards the ground, keeping your back close to the bench or chair. 0639.json#/instructions/3
  5. Pause for a moment at the bottom, then push through your palms to straighten your arms and return to the starting position. 0639.json#/instructions/4
  6. Repeat for the desired number of repetitions, then switch sides and repeat with the other arm. 0639.json#/instructions/5

0640 — one arm slam (with medicine ball)

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 0640 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: source_complete_for_storyboard. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

You lift a medicine ball overhead with one hand and slam it to the floor. Builds explosive power in the shoulders, core and lats.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Stand with your feet shoulder-width apart, holding the medicine ball with one hand in front of your waist. 0640.json#/instructions/0
  2. Bend your knees slightly and engage your core. 0640.json#/instructions/1
  3. Raise the medicine ball above your head, fully extending your arm. 0640.json#/instructions/2
  4. Forcefully slam the medicine ball down to the ground, using your core and shoulders to generate power. 0640.json#/instructions/3
  5. Catch the medicine ball on the bounce and repeat for the desired number of repetitions. 0640.json#/instructions/4

0642 — outside leg kick push-up

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 0642 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: plank.

A push-up where you kick one leg out to the side at the top. Adds core and hip work to the chest and arm effort.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Use a high-plank blocking pose on the documented support, with a long aligned torso; preserve any source-defined low-plank or lifted-leg modification.

authored_from_source_context; source verified: false. Body-pose class uses cited setup/action or title/description context, or an explicitly authored neutral default; detailed placement is a reversible proposal. Basis: source_classification. 0642.json#/instructions/0

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Start in a push-up position with your hands slightly wider than shoulder-width apart and your feet together. 0642.json#/instructions/0
  2. Lower your body towards the ground by bending your elbows, keeping your back straight and core engaged. 0642.json#/instructions/1
  3. As you push back up, kick one leg out to the side, extending it fully and engaging your glutes. 0642.json#/instructions/2
  4. Return your leg to the starting position and repeat the push-up, alternating legs with each repetition. 0642.json#/instructions/3
  5. Continue alternating leg kicks and push-ups for the desired number of repetitions. 0642.json#/instructions/4

0643 — overhead triceps stretch

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 0643 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: seated.

Reaching one arm overhead and bending the elbow, you pull gently on it with the other hand. Stretches the triceps and shoulder.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Stand or sit upright with your feet shoulder-width apart.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 0643.json#/instructions/0

Arms Hands

Rest relaxed hands beside the thighs until the source's arm movement begins; use documented handles or loads when present.

authored_from_source_posture; source verified: false. Initial arm position was not explicit; this is a neutral staging choice compatible with the inferred support pose. 0643.json#/instructions/0

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 0643.json#/instructions/0

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Stand or sit upright with your feet shoulder-width apart. 0643.json#/instructions/0
  2. Extend one arm overhead, bending at the elbow so that your hand reaches towards the opposite shoulder blade. 0643.json#/instructions/1
  3. With your other hand, gently pull the elbow of the extended arm towards the opposite side of your head, feeling a stretch in your triceps. 0643.json#/instructions/2
  4. Hold the stretch for 15-30 seconds, then release. 0643.json#/instructions/3
  5. Repeat on the other side. 0643.json#/instructions/4

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 0648.json#/description

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Start with the barbell on the ground in front of you, with your feet shoulder-width apart. 0648.json#/instructions/0
  2. Bend down and grip the barbell with an overhand grip, slightly wider than shoulder-width apart. 0648.json#/instructions/1
  3. Keep your back straight and chest up as you lift the barbell off the ground, extending your hips and knees. 0648.json#/instructions/2
  4. As the barbell reaches your mid-thigh, explosively pull it upwards, shrugging your shoulders and pulling your elbows high and to the sides. 0648.json#/instructions/3
  5. As the barbell reaches its highest point, quickly drop underneath it, rotating your elbows around and catching the barbell on your shoulders in a front squat position. 0648.json#/instructions/4
  6. Stand up with the barbell, fully extending your hips and knees. 0648.json#/instructions/5
  7. Lower the barbell back down to the starting position, keeping control of the weight throughout the movement. 0648.json#/instructions/6
  8. Repeat for the desired number of repetitions. 0648.json#/instructions/7

Proposed staging

0747 — smith behind neck press

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 0747 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

On the smith machine, you press the bar overhead from behind your neck. Works the shoulders and triceps, so keep it controlled.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Adjust the seat height of the smith machine so that the bar is at shoulder level. Stand with your feet shoulder-width apart and your knees slightly bent. Grasp the bar with an overhand grip, slightly wider than shoulder-width apart.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 0747.json#/instructions/0, 0747.json#/instructions/1, 0747.json#/instructions/2

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 0747.json#/instructions/1

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Adjust the seat height of the smith machine so that the bar is at shoulder level. 0747.json#/instructions/0
  2. Stand with your feet shoulder-width apart and your knees slightly bent. 0747.json#/instructions/1
  3. Grasp the bar with an overhand grip, slightly wider than shoulder-width apart. 0747.json#/instructions/2
  4. Lift the bar off the rack and step back, maintaining a stable stance. 0747.json#/instructions/3
  5. Lower the bar down to the back of your neck, keeping your elbows pointing forward. 0747.json#/instructions/4
  6. Press the bar up overhead until your arms are fully extended. 0747.json#/instructions/5
  7. Pause for a moment at the top, then slowly lower the bar back down to the starting position. 0747.json#/instructions/6
  8. Repeat for the desired number of repetitions. 0747.json#/instructions/7
